# Define vorticity equations for energy flow analysis
def vorticity_equations(r, theta, phi, omega_r, omega_theta, omega_phi):
    """
    Compute vorticity components for toroidal energy flow.
    Parameters:
    - r, theta, phi: Spherical coordinates
    - omega_r, omega_theta, omega_phi: Vorticity components in radial, polar, and azimuthal directions
    Returns:
    - Vorticity vector components
    """
    # Radial, polar, and azimuthal vorticity components
    vorticity_r = sp.diff(omega_theta * sp.sin(phi), phi) - sp.diff(omega_phi, theta)
    vorticity_theta = sp.diff(omega_phi, r) - sp.diff(omega_r * sp.sin(phi), phi)
    vorticity_phi = sp.diff(omega_r * sp.sin(phi), theta) - sp.diff(omega_theta, r)
    
    return [vorticity_r, vorticity_theta, vorticity_phi]

# Define symbolic variables for vorticity analysis
omega_r, omega_theta, omega_phi = sp.symbols("omega_r omega_theta omega_phi")

# Compute vorticity components
vorticity_components = vorticity_equations(r, theta1, phi1, omega_r, omega_theta, omega_phi)

# Simplify the results
vorticity_simplified = [sp.simplify(comp) for comp in vorticity_components]
vorticity_simplified
Result
[omega_theta*cos(phi1), -omega_r*cos(phi1), 0]

# Define stability parameter incorporating vorticity components
def stability_with_vorticity(r, omega_r, omega_theta, alpha, L, turbulence_factor):
    """
    Compute the stability parameter Γ incorporating vorticity components.
    Parameters:
    - r: Radial distance
    - omega_r, omega_theta: Vorticity components in radial and polar directions
    - alpha: Fractal scaling parameter
    - L: Confinement length scale
    - turbulence_factor: Turbulence scaling factor
    Returns:
    - Stability parameter Γ
    """
    # Energy density with fractal scaling and confinement
    energy_density = plasmoid_energy_density(r, alpha, L)
    
    # Vorticity contribution to confinement
    vorticity_confinement = sp.sqrt(omega_r**2 + omega_theta**2)
    
    # Turbulence contribution
    turbulence = turbulence_factor * sp.std(energy_density)
    
    # Compute stability parameter
    stability = vorticity_confinement / (1 + turbulence)
    
    return sp.simplify(stability)

# Correct the plasmoid_energy_density function to use sympy's exp function
def plasmoid_energy_density_corrected(r, alpha, L):
    """
    Compute energy density for plasmoids based on fractal scaling.
    Parameters:
    - r: Radial distance array
    - alpha: Fractal scaling parameter
    - L: Confinement length scale
    Returns:
    - Energy density |Psi|^2
    """
    return (1 / r**alpha) * sp.exp(-r / L)

# Update the stability function with the corrected energy density function
def stability_with_vorticity_corrected(r, omega_r, omega_theta, alpha, L, turbulence_factor):
    """
    Compute the stability parameter Γ incorporating vorticity components.
    Parameters:
    - r: Radial distance
    - omega_r, omega_theta: Vorticity components in radial and polar directions
    - alpha: Fractal scaling parameter
    - L: Confinement length scale
    - turbulence_factor: Turbulence scaling factor
    Returns:
    - Stability parameter Γ
    """
    # Energy density with fractal scaling and confinement
    energy_density = plasmoid_energy_density_corrected(r, alpha, L)
    
    # Vorticity contribution to confinement
    vorticity_confinement = sp.sqrt(omega_r**2 + omega_theta**2)
    
    # Turbulence contribution
    turbulence = turbulence_factor * sp.sqrt(sp.integrate(energy_density, (r, 0, sp.oo)))
    
    # Compute stability parameter
    stability = vorticity_confinement / (1 + turbulence)
    
    return sp.simplify(stability)

# Compute stability parameter with the corrected energy density function

# Simplify the turbulence term for numerical evaluation
def stability_with_vorticity_numeric(r, omega_r, omega_theta, alpha, L, turbulence_factor):
    """
    Numerically compute the stability parameter Γ incorporating vorticity components.
    Parameters:
    - r: Radial distance (numerical value)
    - omega_r, omega_theta: Vorticity components in radial and polar directions
    - alpha: Fractal scaling parameter
    - L: Confinement length scale
    - turbulence_factor: Turbulence scaling factor
    Returns:
    - Numerical stability parameter Γ
    """
    # Energy density with fractal scaling and confinement
    energy_density = (1 / r**alpha) * np.exp(-r / L)
    
    # Vorticity contribution to confinement
    vorticity_confinement = np.sqrt(omega_r**2 + omega_theta**2)
    
    # Simplified turbulence contribution (scaled energy density for numerical evaluation)
    turbulence = turbulence_factor * energy_density
    
    # Compute stability parameter
    stability = vorticity_confinement / (1 + turbulence)
    return stability

# Evaluate the stability parameter numerically for the range of r_values
stability_values_numeric = [
    stability_with_vorticity_numeric(
        r_val, omega_r_value, omega_theta_value, alpha_value, L_value, turbulence_factor_value
    )
    for r_val in r_values
]

# Plot the numerical stability parameter
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))
plt.plot(r_values, stability_values_numeric, label="Stability Parameter (Γ)", color="blue")

# Customize the plot
plt.axhline(1, color="red", linestyle="--", label="Γ = 1 (Stability Threshold)")
plt.xlabel("Radial Distance (r)")
plt.ylabel("Stability Parameter (Γ)")
plt.title("Numerical Evaluation of Stability Parameter (Γ) (Corrected for Turbulence)")
plt.legend()
plt.grid(True)
plt.show()

# Define a grid for visualization of vorticity flow
phi_values = np.linspace(0, 2 * np.pi, 100)  # Azimuthal angle
theta_values = np.linspace(0, np.pi, 100)  # Polar angle
phi_grid, theta_grid = np.meshgrid(phi_values, theta_values)

# Evaluate vorticity components numerically
def vorticity_flow(phi, theta, omega_r, omega_theta):
    """
    Compute vorticity flow components numerically for visualization.
    Parameters:
    - phi: Azimuthal angle
    - theta: Polar angle
    - omega_r: Radial vorticity component
    - omega_theta: Polar vorticity component
    Returns:
    - Vorticity components for radial and polar flow
    """
    vorticity_r = omega_theta * np.cos(phi)  # Radial component
    vorticity_theta = -omega_r * np.cos(phi)  # Polar component
    return vorticity_r, vorticity_theta

# Compute vorticity components over the grid
vorticity_r_grid, vorticity_theta_grid = vorticity_flow(phi_grid, theta_grid, omega_r_value, omega_theta_value)

# Visualize vorticity flow as a quiver plot
plt.figure(figsize=(10, 8))
plt.quiver(
    phi_grid, theta_grid, vorticity_r_grid, vorticity_theta_grid,
    color="blue", scale=20, headwidth=3, alpha=0.75
)
plt.xlabel("Azimuthal Angle (phi)")
plt.ylabel("Polar Angle (theta)")
plt.title("Visualization of Vorticity Flow (Radial and Polar Components)")
plt.grid(True)
plt.show()

# Extend vorticity visualization to 3D to represent the toroidal structure
from mpl_toolkits.mplot3d import Axes3D

# Define a 3D grid for the toroidal structure
phi_3d = np.linspace(0, 2 * np.pi, 30)  # Azimuthal angle
theta_3d = np.linspace(0, np.pi, 30)  # Polar angle
r_3d = np.linspace(1, 2, 10)  # Radial distance (arbitrary scaling for toroidal visualization)
phi_3d_grid, theta_3d_grid, r_3d_grid = np.meshgrid(phi_3d, theta_3d, r_3d)

# Compute 3D vorticity flow components
vorticity_r_3d, vorticity_theta_3d = vorticity_flow(phi_3d_grid, theta_3d_grid, omega_r_value, omega_theta_value)

# Convert spherical to Cartesian coordinates for 3D visualization
x_3d = r_3d_grid * np.sin(theta_3d_grid) * np.cos(phi_3d_grid)
y_3d = r_3d_grid * np.sin(theta_3d_grid) * np.sin(phi_3d_grid)
z_3d = r_3d_grid * np.cos(theta_3d_grid)

# Convert vorticity components to Cartesian directions
vx_3d = vorticity_r_3d * np.sin(theta_3d_grid) * np.cos(phi_3d_grid)
vy_3d = vorticity_r_3d * np.sin(theta_3d_grid) * np.sin(phi_3d_grid)
vz_3d = vorticity_theta_3d * np.cos(theta_3d_grid)

    length=0.2, normalize=True, color="blue", alpha=0.7
)

# Customize the 3D plot
ax.set_xlabel("X")

# Define multi-frequency resonance effects on vorticity flow
def resonance_effects(phi, theta, omega_r, omega_theta, frequencies, amplitudes):
    """
    Compute vorticity flow components with multi-frequency resonance effects.
    Parameters:
    - phi: Azimuthal angle
    - theta: Polar angle
    - omega_r: Radial vorticity component
    - omega_theta: Polar vorticity component
    - frequencies: Array of resonance frequencies
    - amplitudes: Array of resonance amplitudes
    Returns:
    - Vorticity components with resonance-modulated flow
    """
    # Initialize resonance-modulated vorticity components
    vorticity_r_resonance = np.zeros_like(phi)
    vorticity_theta_resonance = np.zeros_like(theta)

    # Add contributions from multiple resonance frequencies
    for freq, amp in zip(frequencies, amplitudes):
        vorticity_r_resonance += amp * np.cos(freq * phi)
        vorticity_theta_resonance += amp * np.sin(freq * theta)
    
    # Combine with base vorticity components
    vorticity_r = omega_theta * np.cos(phi) + vorticity_r_resonance
    vorticity_theta = -omega_r * np.cos(phi) + vorticity_theta_resonance

    return vorticity_r, vorticity_theta

# Define resonance frequencies and amplitudes
resonance_frequencies = [1.0, 2.0, 3.0]  # Example frequencies
resonance_amplitudes = [0.5, 0.3, 0.2]  # Corresponding amplitudes

# Compute vorticity flow with resonance effects over the 3D grid
vorticity_r_resonance, vorticity_theta_resonance = resonance_effects(
    phi_3d_grid, theta_3d_grid, omega_r_value, omega_theta_value, resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes
)

# Convert resonance-modulated vorticity components to Cartesian directions
vx_resonance = vorticity_r_resonance * np.sin(theta_3d_grid) * np.cos(phi_3d_grid)
vy_resonance = vorticity_r_resonance * np.sin(theta_3d_grid) * np.sin(phi_3d_grid)
vz_resonance = vorticity_theta_resonance * np.cos(theta_3d_grid)

# Create 3D quiver plot with resonance effects
fig = plt.figure(figsize=(12, 10))
ax = fig.add_subplot(111, projection='3d')

# Plot the resonance-modulated vorticity flow in 3D
ax.quiver(
    x_3d, y_3d, z_3d, vx_resonance, vy_resonance, vz_resonance,
    length=0.2, normalize=True, color="green", alpha=0.7
)

# Customize the 3D plot
ax.set_xlabel("X")
ax.set_ylabel("Y")
ax.set_zlabel("Z")
ax.set_title("3D Vorticity Flow with Multi-Frequency Resonance Effects")
plt.show()

# Analyze stability trends under multi-frequency resonance
def stability_with_resonance(r, omega_r, omega_theta, alpha, L, turbulence_factor, frequencies, amplitudes):
    """
    Compute the stability parameter Γ incorporating vorticity and multi-frequency resonance.
    Parameters:
    - r: Radial distance
    - omega_r, omega_theta: Vorticity components
    - alpha: Fractal scaling parameter
    - L: Confinement length scale
    - turbulence_factor: Turbulence scaling factor
    - frequencies: Array of resonance frequencies
    - amplitudes: Array of resonance amplitudes
    Returns:
    - Stability parameter Γ with resonance modulation

# Re-import necessary libraries after reset
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

# Redefine the stability function incorporating multi-frequency resonance
def stability_with_resonance(r, omega_r, omega_theta, alpha, L, turbulence_factor, frequencies, amplitudes):
    """
    Compute the stability parameter Γ incorporating vorticity and multi-frequency resonance.
    Parameters:
    - r: Radial distance
    - omega_r, omega_theta: Vorticity components
    - alpha: Fractal scaling parameter
    - L: Confinement length scale
    - turbulence_factor: Turbulence scaling factor
    - frequencies: Array of resonance frequencies
    - amplitudes: Array of resonance amplitudes
    Returns:
    - Stability parameter Γ with resonance modulation
    """
    # Base energy density with fractal scaling and confinement
    energy_density = (1 / r**alpha) * np.exp(-r / L)
    
    # Vorticity contribution
    vorticity_confinement = np.sqrt(omega_r**2 + omega_theta**2)
    
    # Resonance contribution to vorticity
    resonance_effect = sum(
        amp * np.cos(freq * r) for freq, amp in zip(frequencies, amplitudes)
    )
    
    # Modulated vorticity confinement
    modulated_vorticity = vorticity_confinement + resonance_effect
    
    # Turbulence contribution
    turbulence = turbulence_factor * energy_density
    
    # Stability parameter
    stability = modulated_vorticity / (1 + turbulence)
    return stability

# Define parameters for stability analysis
alpha_value = 0.8  # Fractal scaling parameter
L_value = 1.5  # Confinement length scale
omega_r_value = 2.0  # Radial vorticity component
omega_theta_value = 1.5  # Polar vorticity component
turbulence_factor_value = 0.1  # Turbulence scaling factor

# Define resonance frequencies and amplitudes
resonance_frequencies = [1.0, 2.0, 3.0]  # Example frequencies
resonance_amplitudes = [0.5, 0.3, 0.2]  # Corresponding amplitudes

# Define a range of radial distances for evaluation
r_values = np.linspace(0.1, 10, 100)  # Radial distance from 0.1 to 10

# Compute stability trends for a range of radial distances under multi-frequency resonance
stability_trends = [
    stability_with_resonance(
        r_val, omega_r_value, omega_theta_value, alpha_value, L_value, turbulence_factor_value,
        resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes
    )
    for r_val in r_values
]

# Plot stability trends under resonance effects
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))
plt.plot(r_values, stability_trends, label="Stability Parameter (Γ) with Resonance", color="green")

# Customize the plot
plt.axhline(1, color="red", linestyle="--", label="Γ = 1 (Stability Threshold)")
plt.xlabel("Radial Distance (r)")
plt.ylabel("Stability Parameter (Γ)")
plt.title("Stability Trends with Multi-Frequency Resonance Effects")
plt.legend()
plt.grid(True)
plt.show()

# Simulate nested toroidal systems with fractal scaling and resonance

def nested_toroidal_system(r, alpha_values, L_values, omega_r, omega_theta, frequencies, amplitudes):
    """
    Simulate energy density and vorticity interactions for nested toroidal systems.
    Parameters:
    - r: Radial distances
    - alpha_values: Array of fractal scaling parameters for nested layers
    - L_values: Array of confinement length scales for nested layers
    - omega_r, omega_theta: Vorticity components (same for all layers)
    - frequencies, amplitudes: Resonance frequencies and amplitudes for modulation
    Returns:
    - Combined energy density profile and stability trends across layers
    """
    total_energy_density = np.zeros_like(r)
    total_stability = np.zeros_like(r)
    
    # Iterate over nested layers
    for alpha, L in zip(alpha_values, L_values):
        # Compute energy density for this layer
        energy_density = (1 / r**alpha) * np.exp(-r / L)
        
        # Compute resonance-modulated vorticity contributions
        resonance_effect = sum(
            amp * np.cos(freq * r) for freq, amp in zip(frequencies, amplitudes)
        )
        vorticity_confinement = np.sqrt(omega_r**2 + omega_theta**2) + resonance_effect
        
        # Stability parameter for this layer
        turbulence = 0.1 * energy_density  # Simplified turbulence model
        stability = vorticity_confinement / (1 + turbulence)
        
        # Accumulate contributions from this layer
        total_energy_density += energy_density
        total_stability += stability

    return total_energy_density, total_stability

# Parameters for nested systems
alpha_values = [0.8, 1.0, 1.2]  # Fractal scaling parameters for 3 nested layers
L_values = [1.5, 2.0, 2.5]  # Confinement length scales for the layers
frequencies = [1.0, 2.0, 3.0]  # Resonance frequencies
amplitudes = [0.5, 0.3, 0.2]  # Resonance amplitudes

# Radial distances for simulation
r_nested = np.linspace(0.1, 10, 200)

# Simulate nested toroidal systems
energy_density_nested, stability_nested = nested_toroidal_system(
    r_nested, alpha_values, L_values, omega_r_value, omega_theta_value, frequencies, amplitudes
)

# Plot the combined energy density and stability trends
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))

# Energy density plot
plt.subplot(2, 1, 1)
plt.plot(r_nested, energy_density_nested, label="Combined Energy Density", color="blue")
plt.xlabel("Radial Distance (r)")
plt.ylabel("Energy Density")
plt.title("Energy Density Distribution in Nested Toroidal Systems")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

# Stability plot
plt.subplot(2, 1, 2)
plt.plot(r_nested, stability_nested, label="Combined Stability Parameter (Γ)", color="green")
plt.axhline(1, color="red", linestyle="--", label="Γ = 1 (Stability Threshold)")
plt.xlabel("Radial Distance (r)")
plt.ylabel("Stability Parameter (Γ)")
plt.title("Stability Trends in Nested Toroidal Systems")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

plt.tight_layout()
plt.show()

# Simulate inter-layer coupling dynamics in nested toroidal systems
def inter_layer_coupling(r, alpha_values, L_values, omega_r, omega_theta, coupling_factors, frequencies, amplitudes):
    """
    Simulate energy flow and stability trends with inter-layer coupling in nested systems.
    Parameters:
    - r: Radial distances
    - alpha_values: Array of fractal scaling parameters for nested layers
    - L_values: Array of confinement length scales for nested layers
    - omega_r, omega_theta: Vorticity components
    - coupling_factors: Array of coupling strengths between layers
    - frequencies, amplitudes: Resonance frequencies and amplitudes
    Returns:
    - Total energy density and stability trends with coupling effects
    """
    total_energy_density = np.zeros_like(r)
    total_stability = np.zeros_like(r)
    coupling_effects = np.zeros_like(r)

    # Iterate over nested layers and compute inter-layer coupling effects
    for i, (alpha, L) in enumerate(zip(alpha_values, L_values)):
        # Energy density for this layer
        energy_density = (1 / r**alpha) * np.exp(-r / L)
        
        # Resonance-modulated vorticity contributions
        resonance_effect = sum(
            amp * np.cos(freq * r) for freq, amp in zip(frequencies, amplitudes)
        )
        vorticity_confinement = np.sqrt(omega_r**2 + omega_theta**2) + resonance_effect
        
        # Turbulence contribution
        turbulence = 0.1 * energy_density
        
        # Stability parameter for this layer
        stability = vorticity_confinement / (1 + turbulence)
        
        # Coupling effects with previous layer
        if i > 0:
            coupling = coupling_factors[i - 1] * total_energy_density
        else:
            coupling = 0
        
        # Accumulate contributions from this layer, including coupling
        total_energy_density += energy_density + coupling
        total_stability += stability

    return total_energy_density, total_stability

# Parameters for inter-layer coupling simulation
coupling_factors = [0.2, 0.3]  # Coupling strengths between layers

# Simulate inter-layer coupling dynamics
energy_density_coupled, stability_coupled = inter_layer_coupling(
    r_nested, alpha_values, L_values, omega_r_value, omega_theta_value, coupling_factors,
    frequencies, amplitudes
)

# Plot the energy density and stability trends with coupling effects
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))

# Energy density plot with coupling
plt.subplot(2, 1, 1)
plt.plot(r_nested, energy_density_coupled, label="Coupled Energy Density", color="purple")
plt.xlabel("Radial Distance (r)")
plt.ylabel("Energy Density")
plt.title("Energy Density with Inter-Layer Coupling in Nested Toroidal Systems")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

# Stability plot with coupling
plt.subplot(2, 1, 2)
plt.plot(r_nested, stability_coupled, label="Coupled Stability Parameter (Γ)", color="orange")
plt.axhline(1, color="red", linestyle="--", label="Γ = 1 (Stability Threshold)")
plt.xlabel("Radial Distance (r)")
plt.ylabel("Stability Parameter (Γ)")
plt.title("Stability Trends with Inter-Layer Coupling in Nested Toroidal Systems")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

plt.tight_layout()
plt.show()

# Optimize coupling factors for maximum stability and energy coherence
def optimize_coupling_factors(r, alpha_values, L_values, omega_r, omega_theta, frequencies, amplitudes):
    """
    Analyze stability and energy coherence for varying coupling factors.
    Parameters:
    - r: Radial distances
    - alpha_values: Fractal scaling parameters for nested layers
    - L_values: Confinement length scales for nested layers
    - omega_r, omega_theta: Vorticity components
    - frequencies, amplitudes: Resonance frequencies and amplitudes
    Returns:
    - Stability trends and energy densities for varying coupling strengths
    """
    coupling_values = np.linspace(0.1, 0.5, 5)  # Range of coupling factors
    stability_results = []
    energy_density_results = []

    # Iterate over coupling strengths
    for coupling_strength in coupling_values:
        coupling_factors = [coupling_strength] * (len(alpha_values) - 1)  # Uniform coupling between layers

        # Compute energy density and stability with current coupling factors
        energy_density, stability = inter_layer_coupling(
            r, alpha_values, L_values, omega_r, omega_theta, coupling_factors, frequencies, amplitudes
        )
        energy_density_results.append(energy_density)
        stability_results.append(stability)

    return coupling_values, np.array(energy_density_results), np.array(stability_results)

# Perform optimization of coupling factors
coupling_values, energy_density_optimized, stability_optimized = optimize_coupling_factors(
    r_nested, alpha_values, L_values, omega_r_value, omega_theta_value, frequencies, amplitudes
)

# Plot stability trends for varying coupling factors
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))

# Stability plot
for i, coupling_strength in enumerate(coupling_values):
    plt.plot(r_nested, stability_optimized[i], label=f"Coupling Factor = {coupling_strength:.2f}")

plt.xlabel("Radial Distance (r)")

# Simulate dynamic coupling effects in nested toroidal systems
def dynamic_coupling_analysis(r, alpha_values, L_values, omega_r, omega_theta, frequencies, amplitudes, time_steps):
    """
    Simulate stability and energy trends under time-dependent coupling factors.
    Parameters:
    - r: Radial distances
    - alpha_values: Fractal scaling parameters for nested layers
    - L_values: Confinement length scales for nested layers
    - omega_r, omega_theta: Vorticity components
    - frequencies, amplitudes: Resonance frequencies and amplitudes
    - time_steps: Number of time steps for simulation
    Returns:
    - Time-dependent stability and energy density trends
    """
    time = np.linspace(0, 2 * np.pi, time_steps)
    dynamic_stability = []
    dynamic_energy_density = []

    # Simulate for each time step
    for t in time:
        # Dynamic coupling factors as a sinusoidal function of time
        coupling_factors = [0.3 + 0.1 * np.sin(frequencies[0] * t), 0.3 + 0.1 * np.cos(frequencies[1] * t)]

        # Compute energy density and stability with dynamic coupling factors
        energy_density, stability = inter_layer_coupling(
            r, alpha_values, L_values, omega_r, omega_theta, coupling_factors, frequencies, amplitudes
        )
        dynamic_energy_density.append(energy_density)
        dynamic_stability.append(stability)

    return time, np.array(dynamic_energy_density), np.array(dynamic_stability)

# Perform dynamic coupling analysis
time_dynamic, energy_density_dynamic, stability_dynamic = dynamic_coupling_analysis(
    r_nested, alpha_values, L_values, omega_r_value, omega_theta_value, frequencies, amplitudes, time_steps=100
)

# Visualize time-dependent stability trends
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))

# Analyze energy recovery efficiency under dynamic coupling
def energy_recovery_analysis(r, energy_density_dynamic, stability_dynamic):
    """
    Compute energy recovery efficiency based on energy density and stability trends.
    Parameters:
    - r: Radial distances
    - energy_density_dynamic: Time-dependent energy density trends
    - stability_dynamic: Time-dependent stability trends
    Returns:
    - Energy recovery efficiency as a function of time
    """
    # Integrate energy density over all radial distances at each time step
    total_energy_density = np.trapz(energy_density_dynamic, r, axis=1)
    
    # Stability-weighted energy recovery efficiency
    recovery_efficiency = np.mean(stability_dynamic, axis=1) * total_energy_density
    
    return recovery_efficiency

# Compute energy recovery efficiency
recovery_efficiency = energy_recovery_analysis(r_nested, energy_density_dynamic, stability_dynamic)

# Simulate energy recovery and feedback dynamics
def energy_recovery_simulation(time_steps, initial_energy, feedback_fraction, recovery_efficiency, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency):
    """
    Simulate energy recovery and feedback dynamics over time.
    Parameters:
    - time_steps: Number of simulation steps
    - initial_energy: Initial energy available in the system
    - feedback_fraction: Fraction of recovered energy used for feedback
    - recovery_efficiency: Efficiency of the recovery process
    - flux_amplitude: Amplitude of the magnetic flux
    - flux_frequency: Frequency of the magnetic flux oscillations
    Returns:
    - Recovered energy and feedback power over time
    """
    time = np.linspace(0, 2 * np.pi, time_steps)
    recovered_energy = []
    feedback_power = []
    available_energy = initial_energy

    for t in time:
        # Magnetic flux variation
        flux = flux_amplitude * np.cos(flux_frequency * t)

        # Recovered power (inductive recovery)
        power_recovered = recovery_efficiency * np.abs(flux)
        recovered_energy.append(power_recovered)

        # Feedback power
        power_feedback = feedback_fraction * power_recovered
        feedback_power.append(power_feedback)

        # Update available energy with feedback
        available_energy += power_feedback

    return time, np.array(recovered_energy), np.array(feedback_power)

# Define simulation parameters
time_steps = 200
initial_energy = 100  # Arbitrary units
feedback_fraction = 0.3  # 30% of recovered energy used for feedback
recovery_efficiency = 0.8  # 80% efficient recovery process
flux_amplitude = 10  # Amplitude of magnetic flux
flux_frequency = 1.0  # Frequency of magnetic flux oscillations

# Perform the simulation
time_sim, recovered_energy_sim, feedback_power_sim = energy_recovery_simulation(
    time_steps, initial_energy, feedback_fraction, recovery_efficiency, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency
)

# Plot results of the energy recovery simulation
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 6))
plt.plot(time_sim, recovered_energy_sim, label="Recovered Energy", color="blue")
plt.plot(time_sim, feedback_power_sim, label="Feedback Power", color="green")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Energy / Power")
plt.title("Energy Recovery and Feedback Dynamics")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()
plt.show()
Energy Recovery And Feedback Dynamics





This plot illustrates the dynamics of energy recovery and feedback over time in the nested system:

Key Observations:

The recovered energy oscillates in sync with the magnetic flux variations, reflecting the inductive recovery process.

# Extend the energy recovery simulation to include dynamic resonance tuning
def extended_energy_recovery_simulation(
    time_steps, initial_energy, feedback_fraction, recovery_efficiency, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency, resonance_frequency, resonance_amplitude
):
    """
    Extend energy recovery simulation with dynamic resonance tuning.
    Parameters:
    - time_steps: Number of simulation steps
    - initial_energy: Initial energy available in the system
    - feedback_fraction: Fraction of recovered energy used for feedback
    - recovery_efficiency: Efficiency of the recovery process
    - flux_amplitude: Amplitude of the magnetic flux
    - flux_frequency: Frequency of the magnetic flux oscillations
    - resonance_frequency: Frequency of the resonance tuning
    - resonance_amplitude: Amplitude of the resonance tuning effect
    Returns:
    - Recovered energy, feedback power, and resonance-modulated recovery over time
    """
    time = np.linspace(0, 2 * np.pi, time_steps)
    recovered_energy = []
    feedback_power = []
    resonance_modulated_recovery = []
    available_energy = initial_energy

    for t in time:
        # Magnetic flux variation
        flux = flux_amplitude * np.cos(flux_frequency * t)

        # Resonance modulation
        resonance_effect = resonance_amplitude * np.sin(resonance_frequency * t)

        # Recovered power with resonance modulation
        power_recovered = recovery_efficiency * (np.abs(flux) + resonance_effect)
        recovered_energy.append(power_recovered)

        # Feedback power
        power_feedback = feedback_fraction * power_recovered
        feedback_power.append(power_feedback)

        # Update available energy with feedback
        available_energy += power_feedback
        resonance_modulated_recovery.append(available_energy)

    return time, np.array(recovered_energy), np.array(feedback_power), np.array(resonance_modulated_recovery)

# Define additional parameters for resonance tuning
resonance_frequency = 2.0  # Frequency of resonance modulation
resonance_amplitude = 5.0  # Amplitude of resonance effect

# Perform the extended simulation
time_extended, recovered_energy_extended, feedback_power_extended, resonance_recovery_extended = extended_energy_recovery_simulation(
    time_steps, initial_energy, feedback_fraction, recovery_efficiency, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency, resonance_frequency, resonance_amplitude
)

# Plot extended recovery results
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))

# Recovered energy with resonance
plt.subplot(2, 1, 1)
plt.plot(time_extended, recovered_energy_extended, label="Recovered Energy with Resonance", color="blue")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Energy")
plt.title("Recovered Energy with Resonance Tuning")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

# Resonance-modulated recovery
plt.subplot(2, 1, 2)
plt.plot(time_extended, resonance_recovery_extended, label="Resonance-Modulated Recovery", color="purple")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Energy")
plt.title("Resonance-Modulated Total Recovery Over Time")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

plt.tight_layout()
plt.show()
Resonance-Modulated Total Recovery Over Time

1. Recovered Energy with Resonance
Observation:

The recovered energy is enhanced periodically by the resonance effect, reflecting how dynamic tuning amplifies energy capture.

# Optimize resonance parameters (frequency and amplitude) for energy recovery
def optimize_resonance_parameters(
    time_steps, initial_energy, feedback_fraction, recovery_efficiency, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency, resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes
):
    """
    Analyze recovered energy for varying resonance frequencies and amplitudes.
    Parameters:
    - time_steps: Number of simulation steps
    - initial_energy: Initial energy available in the system
    - feedback_fraction: Fraction of recovered energy used for feedback
    - recovery_efficiency: Efficiency of the recovery process
    - flux_amplitude: Amplitude of the magnetic flux
    - flux_frequency: Frequency of the magnetic flux oscillations
    - resonance_frequencies: Array of resonance frequencies to test
    - resonance_amplitudes: Array of resonance amplitudes to test
    Returns:
    - Results as a 2D array of total recovered energy for each parameter combination
    """
    results = []

    for freq in resonance_frequencies:
        row = []
        for amp in resonance_amplitudes:
            # Perform simulation for each frequency and amplitude combination
            _, recovered_energy, _, _ = extended_energy_recovery_simulation(
                time_steps, initial_energy, feedback_fraction, recovery_efficiency, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency, freq, amp
            )
            # Compute total recovered energy over time
            total_energy = np.trapz(recovered_energy)
            row.append(total_energy)
        results.append(row)

    return np.array(results)

# Define range of resonance frequencies and amplitudes for optimization
resonance_frequencies_range = np.linspace(1.0, 5.0, 10)  # From 1 to 5 Hz
resonance_amplitudes_range = np.linspace(1.0, 10.0, 10)  # From 1 to 10 (arbitrary units)

# Perform optimization
optimization_results = optimize_resonance_parameters(
    time_steps, initial_energy, feedback_fraction, recovery_efficiency, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency,
    resonance_frequencies_range, resonance_amplitudes_range
)

# Visualize optimization results
plt.figure(figsize=(10, 8))
plt.contourf(
    resonance_amplitudes_range, resonance_frequencies_range, optimization_results, 20, cmap="viridis"
)
plt.colorbar(label="Total Recovered Energy")
plt.xlabel("Resonance Amplitude")
plt.ylabel("Resonance Frequency (Hz)")
plt.title("Optimization of Resonance Parameters for Energy Recovery")
plt.grid(True)
plt.show()

# Simulate long-term dynamics of the nested toroidal system
def long_term_dynamics_simulation(
    time_steps, initial_energy, feedback_fraction, recovery_efficiency, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency, resonance_frequency, resonance_amplitude, dissipation_rate
):
    """
    Simulate long-term dynamics of energy recovery and stability with dissipation effects.
    Parameters:
    - time_steps: Number of simulation steps
    - initial_energy: Initial energy available in the system
    - feedback_fraction: Fraction of recovered energy used for feedback
    - recovery_efficiency: Efficiency of the recovery process
    - flux_amplitude: Amplitude of the magnetic flux
    - flux_frequency: Frequency of the magnetic flux oscillations
    - resonance_frequency: Frequency of the resonance tuning
    - resonance_amplitude: Amplitude of the resonance tuning effect
    - dissipation_rate: Fraction of energy lost per time step due to dissipation
    Returns:
    - Arrays of recovered energy, feedback power, and total system energy over time
    """
    time = np.linspace(0, 50, time_steps)  # Simulate over a long time span
    recovered_energy = []
    feedback_power = []
    total_system_energy = []

    available_energy = initial_energy

    for t in time:
        # Magnetic flux variation
        flux = flux_amplitude * np.cos(flux_frequency * t)

        # Resonance modulation
        resonance_effect = resonance_amplitude * np.sin(resonance_frequency * t)

        # Recovered power (inductive recovery with resonance effects)
        power_recovered = recovery_efficiency * (np.abs(flux) + resonance_effect)
        recovered_energy.append(power_recovered)

        # Feedback power
        power_feedback = feedback_fraction * power_recovered
        feedback_power.append(power_feedback)

        # Dissipation effect
        energy_loss = dissipation_rate * available_energy

        # Update total system energy
        available_energy = available_energy + power_feedback - energy_loss
        total_system_energy.append(available_energy)

    return time, np.array(recovered_energy), np.array(feedback_power), np.array(total_system_energy)

# Define parameters for the long-term simulation
dissipation_rate = 0.02  # 2% energy loss per time step

# Perform the long-term simulation
time_long_term, recovered_energy_long_term, feedback_power_long_term, total_system_energy_long_term = long_term_dynamics_simulation(
    time_steps=1000,  # Higher resolution for long-term dynamics
    initial_energy=100,  # Initial system energy
    feedback_fraction=0.3,  # Feedback fraction
    recovery_efficiency=0.8,  # Recovery efficiency
    flux_amplitude=10,  # Magnetic flux amplitude
    flux_frequency=1.0,  # Magnetic flux frequency
    resonance_frequency=3.5,  # Optimized resonance frequency
    resonance_amplitude=8.0,  # Optimized resonance amplitude
    dissipation_rate=dissipation_rate  # Energy dissipation rate
)

# Plot long-term system dynamics
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))

# Recovered energy plot
plt.subplot(3, 1, 1)
plt.plot(time_long_term, recovered_energy_long_term, label="Recovered Energy", color="blue")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Energy")
plt.title("Recovered Energy Over Time")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

# Feedback power plot
plt.subplot(3, 1, 2)
plt.plot(time_long_term, feedback_power_long_term, label="Feedback Power", color="green")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Power")
plt.title("Feedback Power Over Time")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

# Total system energy plot
plt.subplot(3, 1, 3)
plt.plot(time_long_term, total_system_energy_long_term, label="Total System Energy", color="purple")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Energy")
plt.title("Total System Energy Over Time (Including Dissipation)")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

plt.tight_layout()
plt.show()
Total System Energy Over Time (Including Dissipation)



This simulation demonstrates the long-term dynamics of the nested toroidal system, including energy recovery, feedback, and dissipation effects:

1. Recovered Energy
Observation:
Recovered energy oscillates consistently over time, driven by magnetic flux variations and resonance effects.
The resonance tuning ensures sustained recovery efficiency even as the system evolves.
2. Feedback Power
Observation:
Feedback power follows a similar oscillatory pattern, proportional to the recovered energy.
Feedback mechanisms dynamically reinforce stability and coherence within the system.
3. Total System Energy
Observation:
Despite dissipation, total system energy stabilizes due to the balance between recovered energy and feedback.
The system maintains a steady-state energy level over the long term, showcasing its robustness.
Key Insights:
Resonance as a Stabilizer:
Optimized resonance parameters dynamically enhance energy recovery and feedback, compensating for dissipation.
Steady-State Operation:
The system achieves a stable energy level over time, highlighting its potential for sustained operation.

# Optimize dissipation rates to analyze their impact on long-term stability
def optimize_dissipation_rates(
    dissipation_rates, time_steps, initial_energy, feedback_fraction, recovery_efficiency, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency, resonance_frequency, resonance_amplitude
):
    """
    Analyze total system energy trends for varying dissipation rates.
    Parameters:
    - dissipation_rates: Array of dissipation rates to test
    - time_steps: Number of simulation steps
    - initial_energy: Initial energy available in the system
    - feedback_fraction: Fraction of recovered energy used for feedback
    - recovery_efficiency: Efficiency of the recovery process
    - flux_amplitude: Amplitude of the magnetic flux
    - flux_frequency: Frequency of the magnetic flux oscillations
    - resonance_frequency: Frequency of the resonance tuning
    - resonance_amplitude: Amplitude of the resonance tuning effect
    Returns:
    - Time array and total system energy trends for each dissipation rate
    """
    total_energy_trends = []

    for dissipation_rate in dissipation_rates:
        # Perform the simulation for each dissipation rate
        _, _, _, total_system_energy = long_term_dynamics_simulation(
            time_steps, initial_energy, feedback_fraction, recovery_efficiency, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency,
            resonance_frequency, resonance_amplitude, dissipation_rate
        )
        total_energy_trends.append(total_system_energy)

    return np.array(total_energy_trends)

# Define a range of dissipation rates to optimize
dissipation_rates_range = np.linspace(0.01, 0.1, 10)  # From 1% to 10% dissipation per time step

# Perform the optimization
total_energy_trends_optimized = optimize_dissipation_rates(
    dissipation_rates_range, time_steps=1000, initial_energy=100, feedback_fraction=0.3, recovery_efficiency=0.8,
    flux_amplitude=10, flux_frequency=1.0, resonance_frequency=3.5, resonance_amplitude=8.0
)

# Plot total system energy trends for varying dissipation rates
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))
for idx, dissipation_rate in enumerate(dissipation_rates_range):
    plt.plot(
        np.linspace(0, 50, 1000),
        total_energy_trends_optimized[idx],
        label=f"Dissipation Rate = {dissipation_rate:.2f}"
    )

plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Total System Energy")
plt.title("Impact of Dissipation Rates on Long-Term Stability")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()
plt.show()

# Simulate advanced feedback mechanisms to counteract high dissipation rates
def advanced_feedback_simulation(
    time_steps, initial_energy, base_feedback_fraction, recovery_efficiency, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency,
    resonance_frequency, resonance_amplitude, dissipation_rate, feedback_adaptation_rate
):
    """
    Simulate long-term dynamics with adaptive feedback based on dissipation.
    Parameters:
    - time_steps: Number of simulation steps
    - initial_energy: Initial energy available in the system
    - base_feedback_fraction: Base fraction of recovered energy used for feedback
    - recovery_efficiency: Efficiency of the recovery process
    - flux_amplitude: Amplitude of the magnetic flux
    - flux_frequency: Frequency of the magnetic flux oscillations
    - resonance_frequency: Frequency of the resonance tuning
    - resonance_amplitude: Amplitude of the resonance tuning effect
    - dissipation_rate: Fraction of energy lost per time step due to dissipation
    - feedback_adaptation_rate: Rate at which feedback fraction increases with dissipation
    Returns:
    - Arrays of recovered energy, feedback power, and total system energy over time
    """
    time = np.linspace(0, 50, time_steps)  # Simulate over a long time span
    recovered_energy = []
    feedback_power = []
    total_system_energy = []

    available_energy = initial_energy

    for t in time:
        # Magnetic flux variation
        flux = flux_amplitude * np.cos(flux_frequency * t)

        # Resonance modulation
        resonance_effect = resonance_amplitude * np.sin(resonance_frequency * t)

        # Recovered power (inductive recovery with resonance effects)
        power_recovered = recovery_efficiency * (np.abs(flux) + resonance_effect)
        recovered_energy.append(power_recovered)

        # Adaptive feedback power
        feedback_fraction = base_feedback_fraction + feedback_adaptation_rate * dissipation_rate
        power_feedback = feedback_fraction * power_recovered
        feedback_power.append(power_feedback)

        # Dissipation effect
        energy_loss = dissipation_rate * available_energy

        # Update total system energy
        available_energy = available_energy + power_feedback - energy_loss
        total_system_energy.append(available_energy)

    return time, np.array(recovered_energy), np.array(feedback_power), np.array(total_system_energy)

# Define parameters for advanced feedback simulation
base_feedback_fraction = 0.3  # Base feedback fraction
feedback_adaptation_rate = 0.5  # Rate of feedback adjustment based on dissipation

# Perform simulation with advanced feedback
time_advanced, recovered_energy_advanced, feedback_power_advanced, total_system_energy_advanced = advanced_feedback_simulation(
    time_steps=1000, initial_energy=100, base_feedback_fraction=base_feedback_fraction,
    recovery_efficiency=0.8, flux_amplitude=10, flux_frequency=1.0, resonance_frequency=3.5,
    resonance_amplitude=8.0, dissipation_rate=0.07, feedback_adaptation_rate=feedback_adaptation_rate
)

# Plot results of advanced feedback simulation
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))

# Recovered energy plot
plt.subplot(3, 1, 1)
plt.plot(time_advanced, recovered_energy_advanced, label="Recovered Energy", color="blue")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Energy")
plt.title("Recovered Energy with Advanced Feedback")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

# Feedback power plot
plt.subplot(3, 1, 2)
plt.plot(time_advanced, feedback_power_advanced, label="Adaptive Feedback Power", color="green")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Power")
plt.title("Feedback Power with Advanced Feedback")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

# Total system energy plot
plt.subplot(3, 1, 3)
plt.plot(time_advanced, total_system_energy_advanced, label="Total System Energy", color="purple")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Energy")
plt.title("Total System Energy with Advanced Feedback and High Dissipation")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

plt.tight_layout()
plt.show()
Total System Energy With Advanced Feedback And High Dissipation

# Simulate the integrated nested toroidal system with practical parameters
def integrated_system_simulation(
    time_steps, initial_energy, feedback_fraction, feedback_adaptation_rate, recovery_efficiency,
    flux_amplitude, flux_frequency, resonance_frequency, resonance_amplitude,
    fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths, dissipation_rate
):
    """
    Simulate the integrated nested toroidal system with practical parameters.
    Parameters:
    - time_steps: Number of simulation steps
    - initial_energy: Initial energy available in the system
    - feedback_fraction: Base feedback fraction of recovered energy
    - feedback_adaptation_rate: Rate of feedback increase based on dissipation
    - recovery_efficiency: Efficiency of the recovery process
    - flux_amplitude: Amplitude of the magnetic flux
    - flux_frequency: Frequency of the magnetic flux oscillations
    - resonance_frequency: Frequency of the resonance tuning
    - resonance_amplitude: Amplitude of the resonance tuning effect
    - fractal_scaling_factors: Fractal scaling factors for nested layers
    - confinement_lengths: Confinement lengths for nested layers
    - dissipation_rate: Fraction of energy lost per time step due to dissipation
    Returns:
    - Arrays of total recovered energy, feedback power, and system energy over time
    """
    time = np.linspace(0, 50, time_steps)
    total_recovered_energy = []
    total_feedback_power = []
    total_system_energy = []

    available_energy = initial_energy

    for t in time:
        # Magnetic flux variation
        flux = flux_amplitude * np.cos(flux_frequency * t)

        # Resonance modulation
        resonance_effect = resonance_amplitude * np.sin(resonance_frequency * t)

        # Accumulate energy and stability contributions across layers
        energy_density_sum = 0
        for alpha, L in zip(fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths):
            layer_energy = (1 / (t + 1)**alpha) * np.exp(-(t + 1) / L)
            energy_density_sum += layer_energy

        # Total recovered energy (inductive recovery with resonance effects)
        power_recovered = recovery_efficiency * (np.abs(flux) + resonance_effect + energy_density_sum)
        total_recovered_energy.append(power_recovered)

        # Adaptive feedback power
        feedback_fraction_dynamic = feedback_fraction + feedback_adaptation_rate * dissipation_rate
        power_feedback = feedback_fraction_dynamic * power_recovered
        total_feedback_power.append(power_feedback)

        # Dissipation effect
        energy_loss = dissipation_rate * available_energy

        # Update total system energy
        available_energy = available_energy + power_feedback - energy_loss
        total_system_energy.append(available_energy)

    return time, np.array(total_recovered_energy), np.array(total_feedback_power), np.array(total_system_energy)

# Define practical parameters for the integrated simulation
fractal_scaling_factors = [0.8, 1.0, 1.2]  # Fractal scaling factors for nested layers
confinement_lengths = [1.5, 2.0, 2.5]  # Confinement lengths for nested layers

# Perform the integrated simulation
time_integrated, recovered_energy_integrated, feedback_power_integrated, total_system_energy_integrated = integrated_system_simulation(
    time_steps=1000, initial_energy=100, feedback_fraction=0.3, feedback_adaptation_rate=0.5,
    recovery_efficiency=0.8, flux_amplitude=10, flux_frequency=1.0, resonance_frequency=3.5,
    resonance_amplitude=8.0, fractal_scaling_factors=fractal_scaling_factors,
    confinement_lengths=confinement_lengths, dissipation_rate=0.07
)

# Plot the integrated system results
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))

# Recovered energy plot
plt.subplot(3, 1, 1)
plt.plot(time_integrated, recovered_energy_integrated, label="Recovered Energy", color="blue")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Energy")
plt.title("Recovered Energy in Integrated System")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

# Feedback power plot
plt.subplot(3, 1, 2)
plt.plot(time_integrated, feedback_power_integrated, label="Feedback Power", color="green")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Power")
plt.title("Feedback Power in Integrated System")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

# Total system energy plot
plt.subplot(3, 1, 3)
plt.plot(time_integrated, total_system_energy_integrated, label="Total System Energy", color="purple")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Energy")
plt.title("Total System Energy in Integrated System")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

plt.tight_layout()
plt.show()

# Simulate dynamic coupling effects in a nested toroidal system with time-varying strength
def dynamic_coupling_simulation(
    time_steps, initial_energy, base_coupling_strength, coupling_variation_amplitude, coupling_frequency,
    fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency, resonance_frequency, resonance_amplitude,
    dissipation_rate, recovery_efficiency
):
    """
    Simulate dynamic coupling effects in a nested toroidal system with time-varying coupling strength.
    Parameters:
    - time_steps: Number of simulation steps
    - initial_energy: Initial energy available in the system
    - base_coupling_strength: Base strength of coupling between layers
    - coupling_variation_amplitude: Amplitude of coupling strength variations
    - coupling_frequency: Frequency of coupling strength oscillations
    - fractal_scaling_factors: Fractal scaling factors for nested layers
    - confinement_lengths: Confinement lengths for nested layers
    - flux_amplitude: Amplitude of the magnetic flux
    - flux_frequency: Frequency of the magnetic flux oscillations
    - resonance_frequency: Frequency of the resonance tuning
    - resonance_amplitude: Amplitude of the resonance tuning effect
    - dissipation_rate: Fraction of energy lost per time step due to dissipation
    - recovery_efficiency: Efficiency of the recovery process
    Returns:
    - Arrays of recovered energy, dynamic coupling effects, and total system energy over time
    """
    time = np.linspace(0, 50, time_steps)
    recovered_energy = []
    dynamic_coupling_effects = []
    total_system_energy = []

    available_energy = initial_energy

    for t in time:
        # Magnetic flux variation
        flux = flux_amplitude * np.cos(flux_frequency * t)

        # Resonance modulation
        resonance_effect = resonance_amplitude * np.sin(resonance_frequency * t)

        # Dynamic coupling strength
        dynamic_coupling_strength = base_coupling_strength + coupling_variation_amplitude * np.sin(coupling_frequency * t)

        # Accumulate energy contributions across nested layers with dynamic coupling
        energy_density_sum = 0
        for alpha, L in zip(fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths):
            layer_energy = (1 / (t + 1)**alpha) * np.exp(-(t + 1) / L)
            energy_density_sum += layer_energy * dynamic_coupling_strength

        # Total recovered energy (inductive recovery with resonance and coupling effects)
        power_recovered = recovery_efficiency * (np.abs(flux) + resonance_effect + energy_density_sum)
        recovered_energy.append(power_recovered)

        # Track dynamic coupling effects
        dynamic_coupling_effects.append(energy_density_sum)

        # Dissipation effect
        energy_loss = dissipation_rate * available_energy

        # Update total system energy
        available_energy = available_energy + power_recovered - energy_loss
        total_system_energy.append(available_energy)

    return time, np.array(recovered_energy), np.array(dynamic_coupling_effects), np.array(total_system_energy)

# Define parameters for dynamic coupling simulation
base_coupling_strength = 0.5  # Base coupling strength
coupling_variation_amplitude = 0.3  # Variation amplitude for coupling strength
coupling_frequency = 0.8  # Frequency of coupling oscillations

# Perform dynamic coupling simulation
time_dynamic_coupling, recovered_energy_dynamic_coupling, coupling_effects_dynamic_coupling, total_energy_dynamic_coupling = dynamic_coupling_simulation(
    time_steps=1000, initial_energy=100, base_coupling_strength=base_coupling_strength,
    coupling_variation_amplitude=coupling_variation_amplitude, coupling_frequency=coupling_frequency,
    fractal_scaling_factors=fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths=confinement_lengths,
    flux_amplitude=10, flux_frequency=1.0, resonance_frequency=3.5, resonance_amplitude=8.0,
    dissipation_rate=0.07, recovery_efficiency=0.8
)

# Plot results of dynamic coupling simulation
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 10))

# Recovered energy plot
plt.subplot(3, 1, 1)
plt.plot(time_dynamic_coupling, recovered_energy_dynamic_coupling, label="Recovered Energy", color="blue")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Energy")
plt.title("Recovered Energy with Dynamic Coupling")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

# Dynamic coupling effects plot
plt.subplot(3, 1, 2)
plt.plot(time_dynamic_coupling, coupling_effects_dynamic_coupling, label="Dynamic Coupling Effects", color="green")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Coupling Contribution")
plt.title("Dynamic Coupling Effects Over Time")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

# Total system energy plot
plt.subplot(3, 1, 3)
plt.plot(time_dynamic_coupling, total_energy_dynamic_coupling, label="Total System Energy", color="purple")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Energy")
plt.title("Total System Energy with Dynamic Coupling")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

plt.tight_layout()
plt.show()
Total System Energy With Dynamic Coupling

# Simulate the interaction between dynamic coupling and resonance effects
def coupling_resonance_interaction_simulation(
    time_steps, initial_energy, base_coupling_strength, coupling_variation_amplitude, coupling_frequency,
    fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency, resonance_frequency_range, resonance_amplitude,
    dissipation_rate, recovery_efficiency
):
    """
    Simulate the interaction between dynamic coupling and resonance effects.
    Parameters:
    - time_steps: Number of simulation steps
    - initial_energy: Initial energy available in the system
    - base_coupling_strength: Base strength of coupling between layers
    - coupling_variation_amplitude: Amplitude of coupling strength variations
    - coupling_frequency: Frequency of coupling strength oscillations
    - fractal_scaling_factors: Fractal scaling factors for nested layers
    - confinement_lengths: Confinement lengths for nested layers
    - flux_amplitude: Amplitude of the magnetic flux
    - flux_frequency: Frequency of the magnetic flux oscillations
    - resonance_frequency_range: Array of resonance frequencies to test
    - resonance_amplitude: Amplitude of the resonance tuning effect
    - dissipation_rate: Fraction of energy lost per time step due to dissipation
    - recovery_efficiency: Efficiency of the recovery process
    Returns:
    - Arrays of total system energy for varying resonance frequencies
    """
    total_energy_results = []

    for resonance_frequency in resonance_frequency_range:
        available_energy = initial_energy
        total_system_energy = []

        for t in np.linspace(0, 50, time_steps):
            # Magnetic flux variation
            flux = flux_amplitude * np.cos(flux_frequency * t)

            # Resonance modulation
            resonance_effect = resonance_amplitude * np.sin(resonance_frequency * t)

            # Dynamic coupling strength
            dynamic_coupling_strength = base_coupling_strength + coupling_variation_amplitude * np.sin(coupling_frequency * t)

            # Accumulate energy contributions across nested layers with dynamic coupling
            energy_density_sum = 0
            for alpha, L in zip(fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths):
                layer_energy = (1 / (t + 1)**alpha) * np.exp(-(t + 1) / L)
                energy_density_sum += layer_energy * dynamic_coupling_strength

            # Total recovered energy (inductive recovery with resonance and coupling effects)
            power_recovered = recovery_efficiency * (np.abs(flux) + resonance_effect + energy_density_sum)

            # Dissipation effect
            energy_loss = dissipation_rate * available_energy

            # Update total system energy
            available_energy = available_energy + power_recovered - energy_loss
            total_system_energy.append(available_energy)

        total_energy_results.append(total_system_energy)

    return np.array(total_energy_results), resonance_frequency_range

# Define a range of resonance frequencies for interaction testing
resonance_frequency_range = np.linspace(1.0, 5.0, 10)  # From 1 Hz to 5 Hz

# Perform coupling-resonance interaction simulation
total_energy_coupling_resonance, resonance_frequency_range_tested = coupling_resonance_interaction_simulation(
    time_steps=1000, initial_energy=100, base_coupling_strength=0.5,
    coupling_variation_amplitude=0.3, coupling_frequency=0.8,
    fractal_scaling_factors=fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths=confinement_lengths,
    flux_amplitude=10, flux_frequency=1.0, resonance_frequency_range=resonance_frequency_range,
    resonance_amplitude=8.0, dissipation_rate=0.07, recovery_efficiency=0.8
)

# Plot total system energy trends for varying resonance frequencies
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))
for idx, resonance_frequency in enumerate(resonance_frequency_range_tested):
    plt.plot(
        np.linspace(0, 50, 1000),
        total_energy_coupling_resonance[idx],
        label=f"Resonance Freq = {resonance_frequency:.1f} Hz"
    )

plt.ylabel("Total System Energy")

plt.legend()

# Simulate multi-frequency resonance effects in a nested toroidal system with dynamic coupling
def multi_frequency_resonance_simulation(
    time_steps, initial_energy, base_coupling_strength, coupling_variation_amplitude, coupling_frequency,
    fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency, resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes,
    dissipation_rate, recovery_efficiency
):
    """
    Simulate multi-frequency resonance effects in a nested toroidal system with dynamic coupling.
    Parameters:
    - time_steps: Number of simulation steps
    - initial_energy: Initial energy available in the system
    - base_coupling_strength: Base strength of coupling between layers
    - coupling_variation_amplitude: Amplitude of coupling strength variations
    - coupling_frequency: Frequency of coupling strength oscillations
    - fractal_scaling_factors: Fractal scaling factors for nested layers
    - confinement_lengths: Confinement lengths for nested layers
    - flux_amplitude: Amplitude of the magnetic flux
    - flux_frequency: Frequency of the magnetic flux oscillations
    - resonance_frequencies: List of resonance frequencies for multi-frequency effects
    - resonance_amplitudes: List of amplitudes corresponding to resonance frequencies
    - dissipation_rate: Fraction of energy lost per time step due to dissipation
    - recovery_efficiency: Efficiency of the recovery process
    Returns:
    - Arrays of recovered energy, resonance contributions, and total system energy over time
    """
    time = np.linspace(0, 50, time_steps)
    recovered_energy = []
    resonance_contributions = []
    total_system_energy = []

    available_energy = initial_energy

    for t in time:
        # Magnetic flux variation
        flux = flux_amplitude * np.cos(flux_frequency * t)

        # Multi-frequency resonance modulation
        resonance_effect = sum(
            amp * np.sin(freq * t) for freq, amp in zip(resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes)
        )

        # Dynamic coupling strength
        dynamic_coupling_strength = base_coupling_strength + coupling_variation_amplitude * np.sin(coupling_frequency * t)

        # Accumulate energy contributions across nested layers with dynamic coupling
        energy_density_sum = 0
        for alpha, L in zip(fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths):
            layer_energy = (1 / (t + 1)**alpha) * np.exp(-(t + 1) / L)
            energy_density_sum += layer_energy * dynamic_coupling_strength

        # Total recovered energy (inductive recovery with resonance and coupling effects)
        power_recovered = recovery_efficiency * (np.abs(flux) + resonance_effect + energy_density_sum)
        recovered_energy.append(power_recovered)

        # Track total resonance contribution
        resonance_contributions.append(resonance_effect)

        # Dissipation effect
        energy_loss = dissipation_rate * available_energy

        # Update total system energy
        available_energy = available_energy + power_recovered - energy_loss
        total_system_energy.append(available_energy)

    return time, np.array(recovered_energy), np.array(resonance_contributions), np.array(total_system_energy)

# Define multiple resonance frequencies and amplitudes for testing
resonance_frequencies_multi = [2.0, 3.5, 4.5]  # Multi-frequency resonance (Hz)
resonance_amplitudes_multi = [5.0, 8.0, 6.0]  # Corresponding amplitudes

# Perform multi-frequency resonance simulation
time_multi_resonance, recovered_energy_multi_resonance, resonance_contributions_multi_resonance, total_energy_multi_resonance = multi_frequency_resonance_simulation(
    time_steps=1000, initial_energy=100, base_coupling_strength=0.5,
    coupling_variation_amplitude=0.3, coupling_frequency=0.8,
    fractal_scaling_factors=fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths=confinement_lengths,
    flux_amplitude=10, flux_frequency=1.0, resonance_frequencies=resonance_frequencies_multi,
    resonance_amplitudes=resonance_amplitudes_multi, dissipation_rate=0.07, recovery_efficiency=0.8
)

# Simulate harmonic and phase effects in multi-frequency resonance
def harmonic_phase_resonance_simulation(
    time_steps, initial_energy, base_coupling_strength, coupling_variation_amplitude, coupling_frequency,
    fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency, resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes,
    resonance_phases, dissipation_rate, recovery_efficiency
):
    """
    Simulate harmonic and phase effects in multi-frequency resonance with dynamic coupling.
    Parameters:
    - time_steps: Number of simulation steps
    - initial_energy: Initial energy available in the system
    - base_coupling_strength: Base strength of coupling between layers
    - coupling_variation_amplitude: Amplitude of coupling strength variations
    - coupling_frequency: Frequency of coupling strength oscillations
    - fractal_scaling_factors: Fractal scaling factors for nested layers
    - confinement_lengths: Confinement lengths for nested layers
    - flux_amplitude: Amplitude of the magnetic flux
    - flux_frequency: Frequency of the magnetic flux oscillations
    - resonance_frequencies: List of resonance frequencies for multi-frequency effects
    - resonance_amplitudes: List of amplitudes corresponding to resonance frequencies
    - resonance_phases: List of phases corresponding to resonance frequencies
    - dissipation_rate: Fraction of energy lost per time step due to dissipation
    - recovery_efficiency: Efficiency of the recovery process
    Returns:
    - Arrays of recovered energy, harmonic resonance contributions, and total system energy over time
    """
    time = np.linspace(0, 50, time_steps)
    recovered_energy = []
    harmonic_resonance_contributions = []
    total_system_energy = []

    available_energy = initial_energy

    for t in time:
        # Magnetic flux variation
        flux = flux_amplitude * np.cos(flux_frequency * t)

        # Multi-frequency resonance modulation with harmonic and phase effects
        resonance_effect = sum(
            amp * np.sin(freq * t + phase) for freq, amp, phase in zip(resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes, resonance_phases)
        )

        # Dynamic coupling strength
        dynamic_coupling_strength = base_coupling_strength + coupling_variation_amplitude * np.sin(coupling_frequency * t)

        # Accumulate energy contributions across nested layers with dynamic coupling
        energy_density_sum = 0
        for alpha, L in zip(fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths):
            layer_energy = (1 / (t + 1)**alpha) * np.exp(-(t + 1) / L)
            energy_density_sum += layer_energy * dynamic_coupling_strength

        # Total recovered energy (inductive recovery with harmonic resonance and coupling effects)
        power_recovered = recovery_efficiency * (np.abs(flux) + resonance_effect + energy_density_sum)
        recovered_energy.append(power_recovered)

        # Track total harmonic resonance contribution
        harmonic_resonance_contributions.append(resonance_effect)

        # Dissipation effect
        energy_loss = dissipation_rate * available_energy

        # Update total system energy
        available_energy = available_energy + power_recovered - energy_loss
        total_system_energy.append(available_energy)

    return time, np.array(recovered_energy), np.array(harmonic_resonance_contributions), np.array(total_system_energy)

# Define harmonic and phase parameters for resonance frequencies
resonance_frequencies_harmonic = [2.0, 3.5, 4.5]  # Resonance frequencies (Hz)
resonance_amplitudes_harmonic = [5.0, 8.0, 6.0]  # Corresponding amplitudes
resonance_phases_harmonic = [0.0, np.pi / 4, np.pi / 2]  # Phase shifts (radians)

# Perform harmonic and phase resonance simulation
time_harmonic_resonance, recovered_energy_harmonic_resonance, harmonic_resonance_contributions, total_energy_harmonic_resonance = harmonic_phase_resonance_simulation(
    time_steps=1000, initial_energy=100, base_coupling_strength=0.5,
    coupling_variation_amplitude=0.3, coupling_frequency=0.8,
    fractal_scaling_factors=fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths=confinement_lengths,
    flux_amplitude=10, flux_frequency=1.0, resonance_frequencies=resonance_frequencies_harmonic,
    resonance_amplitudes=resonance_amplitudes_harmonic, resonance_phases=resonance_phases_harmonic,
    dissipation_rate=0.07, recovery_efficiency=0.8
)

# Plot results of harmonic and phase resonance simulation

# Recovered energy plot

plt.plot(time_harmonic_resonance, recovered_energy_harmonic_resonance, label="Recovered Energy", color="blue")

# Simulate the effect of phase optimization on harmonic resonance contributions
def phase_optimization_simulation(
    time_steps, initial_energy, base_coupling_strength, coupling_variation_amplitude, coupling_frequency,
    fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency, resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes,
    phase_range, dissipation_rate, recovery_efficiency
):
    """
    Simulate the effect of systematically optimizing phase shifts on harmonic resonance contributions.
    Parameters:
    - time_steps: Number of simulation steps
    - initial_energy: Initial energy available in the system
    - base_coupling_strength: Base strength of coupling between layers
    - coupling_variation_amplitude: Amplitude of coupling strength variations
    - coupling_frequency: Frequency of coupling strength oscillations
    - fractal_scaling_factors: Fractal scaling factors for nested layers
    - confinement_lengths: Confinement lengths for nested layers
    - flux_amplitude: Amplitude of the magnetic flux
    - flux_frequency: Frequency of the magnetic flux oscillations
    - resonance_frequencies: List of resonance frequencies for multi-frequency effects
    - resonance_amplitudes: List of amplitudes corresponding to resonance frequencies
    - phase_range: Range of phase shifts to test (in radians)
    - dissipation_rate: Fraction of energy lost per time step due to dissipation
    - recovery_efficiency: Efficiency of the recovery process
    Returns:
    - Arrays of total system energy for varying phase alignments
    """
    total_energy_results = []

    for phase_shift in phase_range:
        resonance_phases = [phase_shift * i for i in range(len(resonance_frequencies))]
        available_energy = initial_energy
        total_system_energy = []

        for t in np.linspace(0, 50, time_steps):
            # Magnetic flux variation
            flux = flux_amplitude * np.cos(flux_frequency * t)

            # Multi-frequency resonance modulation with optimized phase shifts
            resonance_effect = sum(
                amp * np.sin(freq * t + phase) for freq, amp, phase in zip(resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes, resonance_phases)
            )

            # Dynamic coupling strength
            dynamic_coupling_strength = base_coupling_strength + coupling_variation_amplitude * np.sin(coupling_frequency * t)

            # Accumulate energy contributions across nested layers with dynamic coupling
            energy_density_sum = 0
            for alpha, L in zip(fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths):
                layer_energy = (1 / (t + 1)**alpha) * np.exp(-(t + 1) / L)
                energy_density_sum += layer_energy * dynamic_coupling_strength

            # Total recovered energy (inductive recovery with harmonic resonance and coupling effects)
            power_recovered = recovery_efficiency * (np.abs(flux) + resonance_effect + energy_density_sum)

            # Dissipation effect
            energy_loss = dissipation_rate * available_energy

            # Update total system energy
            available_energy = available_energy + power_recovered - energy_loss
            total_system_energy.append(available_energy)

        total_energy_results.append(total_system_energy)

    return np.array(total_energy_results), phase_range

# Define phase range for optimization testing
phase_range_optimization = np.linspace(0, 2 * np.pi, 10)  # From 0 to 2π radians

# Perform phase optimization simulation
total_energy_phase_optimized, phase_range_tested = phase_optimization_simulation(
    time_steps=1000, initial_energy=100, base_coupling_strength=0.5,
    coupling_variation_amplitude=0.3, coupling_frequency=0.8,
    fractal_scaling_factors=fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths=confinement_lengths,
    flux_amplitude=10, flux_frequency=1.0, resonance_frequencies=resonance_frequencies_harmonic,
    resonance_amplitudes=resonance_amplitudes_harmonic, phase_range=phase_range_optimization,
    dissipation_rate=0.07, recovery_efficiency=0.8
)

# Plot total system energy for varying phase alignments
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))
for idx, phase_shift in enumerate(phase_range_tested):
    plt.plot(
        np.linspace(0, 50, 1000),
        total_energy_phase_optimized[idx],
        label=f"Phase Shift = {phase_shift:.2f} radians"
    )

plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Total System Energy")
plt.title("Phase Optimization: Total System Energy for Varying Phase Alignments")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()
plt.show()
Phase Optimization: Total System Energy For Varying Phase Alignments

# Refine phase alignment by simulating finer-grained phase increments
def refine_phase_alignment_simulation(
    time_steps, initial_energy, base_coupling_strength, coupling_variation_amplitude, coupling_frequency,
    fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency, resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes,
    fine_phase_range, dissipation_rate, recovery_efficiency
):
    """
    Refine phase alignment by simulating finer-grained phase increments for harmonic resonance.
    Parameters:
    - time_steps: Number of simulation steps
    - initial_energy: Initial energy available in the system
    - base_coupling_strength: Base strength of coupling between layers
    - coupling_variation_amplitude: Amplitude of coupling strength variations
    - coupling_frequency: Frequency of coupling strength oscillations
    - fractal_scaling_factors: Fractal scaling factors for nested layers
    - confinement_lengths: Confinement lengths for nested layers
    - flux_amplitude: Amplitude of the magnetic flux
    - flux_frequency: Frequency of the magnetic flux oscillations
    - resonance_frequencies: List of resonance frequencies for multi-frequency effects
    - resonance_amplitudes: List of amplitudes corresponding to resonance frequencies
    - fine_phase_range: Finer-grained range of phase shifts (in radians)
    - dissipation_rate: Fraction of energy lost per time step due to dissipation
    - recovery_efficiency: Efficiency of the recovery process
    Returns:
    - Arrays of total system energy for fine phase increments
    """

        available_energy = initial_energy
        total_system_energy = []

            # Magnetic flux variation
            flux = flux_amplitude * np.cos(flux_frequency * t)

            # Multi-frequency resonance modulation with refined phase shifts
            resonance_effect = sum(
                amp * np.sin(freq * t + phase) for freq, amp, phase in zip(resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes, resonance_phases)
            )

            # Dynamic coupling strength

            # Dissipation effect
            energy_loss = dissipation_rate * available_energy

            # Update total system energy
            available_energy = available_energy + power_recovered - energy_loss
            total_system_energy.append(available_energy)

        total_energy_results.append(total_system_energy)

    return np.array(total_energy_results), fine_phase_range
