# Simulate dynamic phase control for real-time phase alignment optimization
def dynamic_phase_control_simulation(
    time_steps, initial_energy, base_coupling_strength, coupling_variation_amplitude, coupling_frequency,
    fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency, resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes,
    dissipation_rate, recovery_efficiency, phase_control_rate
):
    """
    Simulate dynamic phase control to maintain real-time optimal alignment.
    Parameters:
    - time_steps: Number of simulation steps
    - initial_energy: Initial energy available in the system
    - base_coupling_strength: Base strength of coupling between layers
    - coupling_variation_amplitude: Amplitude of coupling strength variations
    - coupling_frequency: Frequency of coupling strength oscillations
    - fractal_scaling_factors: Fractal scaling factors for nested layers
    - confinement_lengths: Confinement lengths for nested layers
    - flux_amplitude: Amplitude of the magnetic flux
    - flux_frequency: Frequency of the magnetic flux oscillations
    - resonance_frequencies: List of resonance frequencies for multi-frequency effects
    - resonance_amplitudes: List of amplitudes corresponding to resonance frequencies
    - dissipation_rate: Fraction of energy lost per time step due to dissipation
    - recovery_efficiency: Efficiency of the recovery process
    - phase_control_rate: Rate of dynamic phase adjustment per time step
    Returns:
    - Arrays of recovered energy, dynamic phase contributions, and total system energy over time
    """
    time = np.linspace(0, 50, time_steps)
    recovered_energy = []
    dynamic_phase_contributions = []
    total_system_energy = []

    available_energy = initial_energy
    dynamic_phases = [0.0] * len(resonance_frequencies)  # Initialize phases

    for t in time:
        # Magnetic flux variation
        flux = flux_amplitude * np.cos(flux_frequency * t)

        # Update dynamic phases for real-time alignment
        for i in range(len(dynamic_phases)):
            dynamic_phases[i] += phase_control_rate * np.sin(t)  # Adjust dynamically

        # Multi-frequency resonance modulation with dynamic phases
        resonance_effect = sum(
            amp * np.sin(freq * t + phase) for freq, amp, phase in zip(resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes, dynamic_phases)
        )

        # Dynamic coupling strength
        dynamic_coupling_strength = base_coupling_strength + coupling_variation_amplitude * np.sin(coupling_frequency * t)

        # Accumulate energy contributions across nested layers with dynamic coupling
        energy_density_sum = 0
        for alpha, L in zip(fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths):
            layer_energy = (1 / (t + 1)**alpha) * np.exp(-(t + 1) / L)
            energy_density_sum += layer_energy * dynamic_coupling_strength

        # Total recovered energy (inductive recovery with harmonic resonance and coupling effects)
        power_recovered = recovery_efficiency * (np.abs(flux) + resonance_effect + energy_density_sum)
        recovered_energy.append(power_recovered)

        # Track dynamic phase contributions
        dynamic_phase_contributions.append(resonance_effect)

        # Dissipation effect
        energy_loss = dissipation_rate * available_energy

        # Update total system energy
        available_energy = available_energy + power_recovered - energy_loss
        total_system_energy.append(available_energy)

    return time, np.array(recovered_energy), np.array(dynamic_phase_contributions), np.array(total_system_energy)

# Define parameters for dynamic phase control simulation
phase_control_rate = 0.1  # Rate of dynamic phase adjustment per time step

# Perform dynamic phase control simulation
time_dynamic_phase, recovered_energy_dynamic_phase, dynamic_phase_contributions, total_energy_dynamic_phase = dynamic_phase_control_simulation(
    time_steps=1000, initial_energy=100, base_coupling_strength=0.5,
    coupling_variation_amplitude=0.3, coupling_frequency=0.8,
    fractal_scaling_factors=fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths=confinement_lengths,
    flux_amplitude=10, flux_frequency=1.0, resonance_frequencies=resonance_frequencies_harmonic,
    resonance_amplitudes=resonance_amplitudes_harmonic, dissipation_rate=0.07,
    recovery_efficiency=0.8, phase_control_rate=phase_control_rate
)

# Plot results of dynamic phase control simulation
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 10))

# Recovered energy plot
plt.subplot(3, 1, 1)
plt.plot(time_dynamic_phase, recovered_energy_dynamic_phase, label="Recovered Energy", color="blue")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Energy")
plt.title("Recovered Energy with Dynamic Phase Control")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

# Dynamic phase contributions plot
plt.subplot(3, 1, 2)
plt.plot(time_dynamic_phase, dynamic_phase_contributions, label="Dynamic Phase Contributions", color="green")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Phase Contribution")
plt.title("Dynamic Phase Contributions Over Time")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

# Total system energy plot
plt.subplot(3, 1, 3)
plt.plot(time_dynamic_phase, total_energy_dynamic_phase, label="Total System Energy", color="purple")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Energy")
plt.title("Total System Energy with Dynamic Phase Control")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

plt.tight_layout()
plt.show()
Total System Energy With Dynamic Phase Control

# Simulate robustness of dynamic phase control under external disturbances
def robustness_testing_dynamic_phase_control(
    time_steps, initial_energy, base_coupling_strength, coupling_variation_amplitude, coupling_frequency,
    fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency, resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes,
    dissipation_rate, recovery_efficiency, phase_control_rate, disturbance_amplitude
):
    """
    Test robustness of dynamic phase control under external disturbances.
    Parameters:
    - time_steps: Number of simulation steps
    - initial_energy: Initial energy available in the system
    - base_coupling_strength: Base strength of coupling between layers
    - coupling_variation_amplitude: Amplitude of coupling strength variations
    - coupling_frequency: Frequency of coupling strength oscillations
    - fractal_scaling_factors: Fractal scaling factors for nested layers
    - confinement_lengths: Confinement lengths for nested layers
    - flux_amplitude: Amplitude of the magnetic flux
    - flux_frequency: Frequency of the magnetic flux oscillations
    - resonance_frequencies: List of resonance frequencies for multi-frequency effects
    - resonance_amplitudes: List of amplitudes corresponding to resonance frequencies
    - dissipation_rate: Fraction of energy lost per time step due to dissipation
    - recovery_efficiency: Efficiency of the recovery process
    - phase_control_rate: Rate of dynamic phase adjustment per time step
    - disturbance_amplitude: Amplitude of random external disturbances
    Returns:
    - Arrays of recovered energy, dynamic phase contributions, and total system energy over time
    """
    time = np.linspace(0, 50, time_steps)
    recovered_energy = []
    dynamic_phase_contributions = []
    total_system_energy = []

    available_energy = initial_energy
    dynamic_phases = [0.0] * len(resonance_frequencies)  # Initialize phases

    for t in time:
        # Magnetic flux variation with external disturbance
        flux = flux_amplitude * np.cos(flux_frequency * t) + disturbance_amplitude * np.random.uniform(-1, 1)

        # Update dynamic phases for real-time alignment
        for i in range(len(dynamic_phases)):
            dynamic_phases[i] += phase_control_rate * np.sin(t)  # Adjust dynamically

        # Multi-frequency resonance modulation with dynamic phases
        resonance_effect = sum(
            amp * np.sin(freq * t + phase) for freq, amp, phase in zip(resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes, dynamic_phases)
        )

        # Dynamic coupling strength
        dynamic_coupling_strength = base_coupling_strength + coupling_variation_amplitude * np.sin(coupling_frequency * t)

        # Accumulate energy contributions across nested layers with dynamic coupling
        energy_density_sum = 0
        for alpha, L in zip(fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths):
            layer_energy = (1 / (t + 1)**alpha) * np.exp(-(t + 1) / L)
            energy_density_sum += layer_energy * dynamic_coupling_strength

        # Total recovered energy (inductive recovery with harmonic resonance and coupling effects)
        power_recovered = recovery_efficiency * (np.abs(flux) + resonance_effect + energy_density_sum)
        recovered_energy.append(power_recovered)

        # Track dynamic phase contributions
        dynamic_phase_contributions.append(resonance_effect)

        # Dissipation effect
        energy_loss = dissipation_rate * available_energy

        # Update total system energy
        available_energy = available_energy + power_recovered - energy_loss
        total_system_energy.append(available_energy)

    return time, np.array(recovered_energy), np.array(dynamic_phase_contributions), np.array(total_system_energy)

# Define disturbance amplitude for robustness testing
disturbance_amplitude = 2.0  # External disturbance amplitude

# Perform robustness testing with dynamic phase control
time_robustness, recovered_energy_robustness, dynamic_phase_contributions_robustness, total_energy_robustness = robustness_testing_dynamic_phase_control(
    time_steps=1000, initial_energy=100, base_coupling_strength=0.5,
    coupling_variation_amplitude=0.3, coupling_frequency=0.8,
    fractal_scaling_factors=fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths=confinement_lengths,
    flux_amplitude=10, flux_frequency=1.0, resonance_frequencies=resonance_frequencies_harmonic,
    resonance_amplitudes=resonance_amplitudes_harmonic, dissipation_rate=0.07,
    recovery_efficiency=0.8, phase_control_rate=phase_control_rate, disturbance_amplitude=disturbance_amplitude
)

# Simulate the effect of feedback tuning under severe disturbances
def feedback_tuning_simulation(
    time_steps, initial_energy, base_coupling_strength, coupling_variation_amplitude, coupling_frequency,
    fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency, resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes,
    dissipation_rate, recovery_efficiency, phase_control_rate, disturbance_amplitude, feedback_tuning_rate
):
    """
    Simulate feedback tuning to counteract severe disturbances and enhance system stability.
    Parameters:
    - time_steps: Number of simulation steps
    - initial_energy: Initial energy available in the system
    - base_coupling_strength: Base strength of coupling between layers
    - coupling_variation_amplitude: Amplitude of coupling strength variations
    - coupling_frequency: Frequency of coupling strength oscillations
    - fractal_scaling_factors: Fractal scaling factors for nested layers
    - confinement_lengths: Confinement lengths for nested layers
    - flux_amplitude: Amplitude of the magnetic flux
    - flux_frequency: Frequency of the magnetic flux oscillations
    - resonance_frequencies: List of resonance frequencies for multi-frequency effects
    - resonance_amplitudes: List of amplitudes corresponding to resonance frequencies
    - dissipation_rate: Fraction of energy lost per time step due to dissipation
    - recovery_efficiency: Efficiency of the recovery process
    - phase_control_rate: Rate of dynamic phase adjustment per time step
    - disturbance_amplitude: Amplitude of random external disturbances
    - feedback_tuning_rate: Rate of feedback adjustment in response to disturbances
    Returns:
    - Arrays of recovered energy, dynamic phase contributions, feedback effects, and total system energy over time
    """
    time = np.linspace(0, 50, time_steps)
    recovered_energy = []
    feedback_effects = []
    dynamic_phase_contributions = []
    total_system_energy = []

    available_energy = initial_energy
    dynamic_phases = [0.0] * len(resonance_frequencies)  # Initialize phases

    for t in time:
        # Magnetic flux variation with external disturbance
        flux = flux_amplitude * np.cos(flux_frequency * t) + disturbance_amplitude * np.random.uniform(-1, 1)

        # Update dynamic phases for real-time alignment
        for i in range(len(dynamic_phases)):
            dynamic_phases[i] += phase_control_rate * np.sin(t)  # Adjust dynamically

        # Multi-frequency resonance modulation with dynamic phases
        resonance_effect = sum(
            amp * np.sin(freq * t + phase) for freq, amp, phase in zip(resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes, dynamic_phases)
        )

        # Dynamic coupling strength
        dynamic_coupling_strength = base_coupling_strength + coupling_variation_amplitude * np.sin(coupling_frequency * t)

        # Feedback adjustment based on disturbance
        feedback_adjustment = feedback_tuning_rate * np.abs(disturbance_amplitude * np.random.uniform(-1, 1))
        feedback_effect = recovery_efficiency * feedback_adjustment
        feedback_effects.append(feedback_effect)

        # Accumulate energy contributions across nested layers with dynamic coupling
        energy_density_sum = 0
        for alpha, L in zip(fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths):
            layer_energy = (1 / (t + 1)**alpha) * np.exp(-(t + 1) / L)
            energy_density_sum += layer_energy * (dynamic_coupling_strength + feedback_effect)

        # Total recovered energy (inductive recovery with harmonic resonance and coupling effects)
        power_recovered = recovery_efficiency * (np.abs(flux) + resonance_effect + energy_density_sum)
        recovered_energy.append(power_recovered)

        # Track dynamic phase contributions
        dynamic_phase_contributions.append(resonance_effect)

        # Dissipation effect
        energy_loss = dissipation_rate * available_energy

        # Update total system energy
        available_energy = available_energy + power_recovered - energy_loss
        total_system_energy.append(available_energy)

    return time, np.array(recovered_energy), np.array(dynamic_phase_contributions), np.array(feedback_effects), np.array(total_system_energy)

# Define feedback tuning rate for robustness testing
feedback_tuning_rate = 0.2  # Feedback adjustment rate

# Perform feedback tuning simulation
time_feedback_tuning, recovered_energy_feedback_tuning, dynamic_phase_contributions_feedback_tuning, feedback_effects_tuning, total_energy_feedback_tuning = feedback_tuning_simulation(
    time_steps=1000, initial_energy=100, base_coupling_strength=0.5,
    coupling_variation_amplitude=0.3, coupling_frequency=0.8,
    fractal_scaling_factors=fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths=confinement_lengths,
    flux_amplitude=10, flux_frequency=1.0, resonance_frequencies=resonance_frequencies_harmonic,
    resonance_amplitudes=resonance_amplitudes_harmonic, dissipation_rate=0.07,
    recovery_efficiency=0.8, phase_control_rate=phase_control_rate,
    disturbance_amplitude=severe_disturbance_amplitude, feedback_tuning_rate=feedback_tuning_rate
)

# Plot results of feedback tuning simulation
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 12))

# Recovered energy plot

# Simulate energy buffering mechanism for stabilization under extreme disturbances
def energy_buffering_simulation(
    time_steps, initial_energy, base_coupling_strength, coupling_variation_amplitude, coupling_frequency,
    fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency, resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes,
    dissipation_rate, recovery_efficiency, phase_control_rate, disturbance_amplitude, buffer_capacity, buffer_discharge_rate
):
    """
    Simulate energy buffering to stabilize total system energy under extreme disturbances.
    Parameters:
    - time_steps: Number of simulation steps
    - initial_energy: Initial energy available in the system
    - base_coupling_strength: Base strength of coupling between layers
    - coupling_variation_amplitude: Amplitude of coupling strength variations
    - coupling_frequency: Frequency of coupling strength oscillations
    - fractal_scaling_factors: Fractal scaling factors for nested layers
    - confinement_lengths: Confinement lengths for nested layers
    - flux_amplitude: Amplitude of the magnetic flux
    - flux_frequency: Frequency of the magnetic flux oscillations
    - resonance_frequencies: List of resonance frequencies for multi-frequency effects
    - resonance_amplitudes: List of amplitudes corresponding to resonance frequencies
    - dissipation_rate: Fraction of energy lost per time step due to dissipation
    - recovery_efficiency: Efficiency of the recovery process
    - phase_control_rate: Rate of dynamic phase adjustment per time step
    - disturbance_amplitude: Amplitude of random external disturbances
    - buffer_capacity: Maximum energy the buffer can hold
    - buffer_discharge_rate: Rate at which the buffer releases energy during dips
    Returns:
    - Arrays of recovered energy, buffer energy levels, and total system energy over time
    """
    time = np.linspace(0, 50, time_steps)
    recovered_energy = []
    buffer_energy_levels = []
    total_system_energy = []

    available_energy = initial_energy
    buffer_energy = 0.0
    dynamic_phases = [0.0] * len(resonance_frequencies)  # Initialize phases

    for t in time:
        # Magnetic flux variation with external disturbance
        flux = flux_amplitude * np.cos(flux_frequency * t) + disturbance_amplitude * np.random.uniform(-1, 1)

        # Update dynamic phases for real-time alignment
        for i in range(len(dynamic_phases)):
            dynamic_phases[i] += phase_control_rate * np.sin(t)  # Adjust dynamically

        # Multi-frequency resonance modulation with dynamic phases
        resonance_effect = sum(
            amp * np.sin(freq * t + phase) for freq, amp, phase in zip(resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes, dynamic_phases)
        )

        # Dynamic coupling strength
        dynamic_coupling_strength = base_coupling_strength + coupling_variation_amplitude * np.sin(coupling_frequency * t)

        # Accumulate energy contributions across nested layers with dynamic coupling
        energy_density_sum = 0
        for alpha, L in zip(fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths):
            layer_energy = (1 / (t + 1)**alpha) * np.exp(-(t + 1) / L)
            energy_density_sum += layer_energy * dynamic_coupling_strength

        # Total recovered energy (inductive recovery with harmonic resonance and coupling effects)
        power_recovered = recovery_efficiency * (np.abs(flux) + resonance_effect + energy_density_sum)
        recovered_energy.append(power_recovered)

        # Manage energy buffer
        if power_recovered > available_energy and buffer_energy < buffer_capacity:
            buffer_energy += power_recovered - available_energy  # Store excess energy
        elif power_recovered < available_energy and buffer_energy > 0:
            buffer_energy -= buffer_discharge_rate  # Release energy to stabilize

        buffer_energy = max(0, min(buffer_energy, buffer_capacity))  # Clamp buffer energy within limits
        buffer_energy_levels.append(buffer_energy)

        # Dissipation effect
        energy_loss = dissipation_rate * available_energy

        # Update total system energy
        available_energy = available_energy + power_recovered - energy_loss + buffer_energy
        total_system_energy.append(available_energy)

    return time, np.array(recovered_energy), np.array(buffer_energy_levels), np.array(total_system_energy)

# Define energy buffer parameters
buffer_capacity = 50.0  # Maximum buffer capacity
buffer_discharge_rate = 2.0  # Buffer discharge rate

# Perform energy buffering simulation

    fractal_scaling_factors=fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths=confinement_lengths,
    flux_amplitude=10, flux_frequency=1.0, resonance_frequencies=resonance_frequencies_harmonic,

# Optimize buffer parameters: simulate varying buffer capacities and discharge rates
def buffer_parameter_optimization(
    buffer_capacities, discharge_rates, time_steps, initial_energy, base_coupling_strength,
    coupling_variation_amplitude, coupling_frequency, fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths,
    flux_amplitude, flux_frequency, resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes, dissipation_rate,
    recovery_efficiency, phase_control_rate, disturbance_amplitude
):
    """
    Optimize buffer parameters by testing different capacities and discharge rates.
    Parameters:
    - buffer_capacities: List of buffer capacities to test
    - discharge_rates: List of discharge rates to test
    Returns:
    - Results for total system energy across different buffer configurations
    """
    optimization_results = {}

    for buffer_capacity in buffer_capacities:
        for discharge_rate in discharge_rates:
            # Run simulation for each combination of buffer capacity and discharge rate
            _, _, _, total_energy = energy_buffering_simulation(
                time_steps=time_steps, initial_energy=initial_energy, base_coupling_strength=base_coupling_strength,
                coupling_variation_amplitude=coupling_variation_amplitude, coupling_frequency=coupling_frequency,
                fractal_scaling_factors=fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths=confinement_lengths,
                flux_amplitude=flux_amplitude, flux_frequency=flux_frequency,
                resonance_frequencies=resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes=resonance_amplitudes,
                dissipation_rate=dissipation_rate, recovery_efficiency=recovery_efficiency,
                phase_control_rate=phase_control_rate, disturbance_amplitude=disturbance_amplitude,
                buffer_capacity=buffer_capacity, buffer_discharge_rate=discharge_rate
            )
            optimization_results[(buffer_capacity, discharge_rate)] = total_energy[-1]  # Store final energy level

    return optimization_results

# Define ranges for buffer capacity and discharge rate
buffer_capacities = [30, 50, 70]  # Test buffer capacities
discharge_rates = [1.0, 2.0, 3.0]  # Test buffer discharge rates

# Perform buffer parameter optimization
buffer_optimization_results = buffer_parameter_optimization(
    buffer_capacities=buffer_capacities, discharge_rates=discharge_rates,
    time_steps=1000, initial_energy=100, base_coupling_strength=0.5,
    coupling_variation_amplitude=0.3, coupling_frequency=0.8,
    fractal_scaling_factors=fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths=confinement_lengths,
    flux_amplitude=10, flux_frequency=1.0, resonance_frequencies=resonance_frequencies_harmonic,
    resonance_amplitudes=resonance_amplitudes_harmonic, dissipation_rate=0.07,
    recovery_efficiency=0.8, phase_control_rate=phase_control_rate,
    disturbance_amplitude=extreme_disturbance_amplitude
)

Interpretation of Buffer Optimization Results

# Simulate combined supplemental mechanisms: energy buffering, phase delay compensation, and multi-mode feedback
def combined_mechanisms_simulation(
    time_steps, initial_energy, base_coupling_strength, coupling_variation_amplitude, coupling_frequency,
    fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency, resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes,
    dissipation_rate, recovery_efficiency, phase_control_rate, disturbance_amplitude,
    buffer_capacity, buffer_discharge_rate, phase_delay_rate, fast_feedback_rate, slow_feedback_rate
):
    """
    Simulate the combined effect of energy buffering, phase delay compensation, and multi-mode feedback.
    Parameters:
    - buffer_capacity: Maximum energy the buffer can hold
    - buffer_discharge_rate: Rate at which the buffer releases energy during dips
    - phase_delay_rate: Rate of phase delay compensation
    - fast_feedback_rate: Fast-acting feedback loop rate for high-frequency disturbances
    - slow_feedback_rate: Slow feedback loop rate for overall stabilization
    Returns:
    - Arrays of recovered energy, buffer energy levels, feedback effects, and total system energy over time
    """
    time = np.linspace(0, 50, time_steps)
    recovered_energy = []
    buffer_energy_levels = []
    feedback_effects = []
    total_system_energy = []

    available_energy = initial_energy
    buffer_energy = 0.0
    dynamic_phases = [0.0] * len(resonance_frequencies)  # Initialize phases

    for t in time:
        # Magnetic flux variation with external disturbance
        flux = flux_amplitude * np.cos(flux_frequency * t) + disturbance_amplitude * np.random.uniform(-1, 1)

        # Update dynamic phases for real-time alignment with phase delay compensation
        for i in range(len(dynamic_phases)):
            phase_adjustment = phase_control_rate * np.sin(t) - phase_delay_rate * np.sin(2 * t)
            dynamic_phases[i] += phase_adjustment  # Include phase delay compensation

        # Multi-frequency resonance modulation with adjusted phases
        resonance_effect = sum(
            amp * np.sin(freq * t + phase) for freq, amp, phase in zip(resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes, dynamic_phases)
        )

        # Dynamic coupling strength
        dynamic_coupling_strength = base_coupling_strength + coupling_variation_amplitude * np.sin(coupling_frequency * t)

        # Feedback loops
        fast_feedback = fast_feedback_rate * np.abs(flux)
        slow_feedback = slow_feedback_rate * (available_energy - 100)  # Relative stabilization around 100
        feedback_effect = fast_feedback + slow_feedback
        feedback_effects.append(feedback_effect)

        # Accumulate energy contributions across nested layers with dynamic coupling and feedback
        energy_density_sum = 0
        for alpha, L in zip(fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths):
            layer_energy = (1 / (t + 1)**alpha) * np.exp(-(t + 1) / L)
            energy_density_sum += layer_energy * (dynamic_coupling_strength + feedback_effect)

        # Total recovered energy (inductive recovery with harmonic resonance and combined effects)
        power_recovered = recovery_efficiency * (np.abs(flux) + resonance_effect + energy_density_sum)
        recovered_energy.append(power_recovered)

        # Manage energy buffer
        if power_recovered > available_energy and buffer_energy < buffer_capacity:
            buffer_energy += power_recovered - available_energy  # Store excess energy
        elif power_recovered < available_energy and buffer_energy > 0:
            buffer_energy -= buffer_discharge_rate  # Release energy to stabilize

        buffer_energy = max(0, min(buffer_energy, buffer_capacity))  # Clamp buffer energy within limits
        buffer_energy_levels.append(buffer_energy)

        # Dissipation effect
        energy_loss = dissipation_rate * available_energy

        # Update total system energy
        available_energy = available_energy + power_recovered - energy_loss + buffer_energy
        total_system_energy.append(available_energy)

    return time, np.array(recovered_energy), np.array(buffer_energy_levels), np.array(feedback_effects), np.array(total_system_energy)


# Define combined mechanism parameters
phase_delay_rate = 0.05  # Phase delay compensation rate
fast_feedback_rate = 0.1  # Fast feedback loop rate
slow_feedback_rate = 0.02  # Slow feedback loop rate

# Perform combined mechanisms simulation
time_combined, recovered_energy_combined, buffer_energy_levels_combined, feedback_effects_combined, total_energy_combined = combined_mechanisms_simulation(
    time_steps=1000, initial_energy=100, base_coupling_strength=0.5,
    coupling_variation_amplitude=0.3, coupling_frequency=0.8,
    fractal_scaling_factors=fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths=confinement_lengths,
    flux_amplitude=10, flux_frequency=1.0, resonance_frequencies=resonance_frequencies_harmonic,
    resonance_amplitudes=resonance_amplitudes_harmonic, dissipation_rate=0.07,
    recovery_efficiency=0.8, phase_control_rate=phase_control_rate,
    disturbance_amplitude=extreme_disturbance_amplitude, buffer_capacity=50,
    buffer_discharge_rate=2.0, phase_delay_rate=phase_delay_rate,
    fast_feedback_rate=fast_feedback_rate, slow_feedback_rate=slow_feedback_rate
)

# Plot results of combined mechanisms simulation
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 12))

# Recovered energy plot
plt.subplot(4, 1, 1)
plt.plot(time_combined, recovered_energy_combined, label="Recovered Energy", color="blue")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Energy")
plt.title("Recovered Energy with Combined Supplemental Mechanisms")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

# Buffer energy levels plot

plt.plot(time_combined, buffer_energy_levels_combined, label="Buffer Energy Levels", color="orange")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Energy")
plt.title("Energy Buffer Levels with Combined Mechanisms")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

# Feedback effects plot
plt.subplot(4, 1, 3)
plt.plot(time_combined, feedback_effects_combined, label="Feedback Effects", color="green")
plt.xlabel("Time")
plt.ylabel("Feedback")
plt.title("Feedback Effects Over Time with Combined Mechanisms")
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

plt.plot(time_combined, total_energy_combined, label="Total System Energy", color="purple")

plt.ylabel("Energy")
plt.title("Total System Energy with Combined Supplemental Mechanisms")

# Test long-term behavior of the system with combined supplemental mechanisms
def long_term_behavior_simulation(
    total_time, time_steps_per_unit, initial_energy, base_coupling_strength, coupling_variation_amplitude,
    coupling_frequency, fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths, flux_amplitude, flux_frequency,
    resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes, dissipation_rate, recovery_efficiency, phase_control_rate,
    disturbance_amplitude, buffer_capacity, buffer_discharge_rate, phase_delay_rate, fast_feedback_rate, slow_feedback_rate
):
    """
    Simulate the long-term behavior of the system with combined supplemental mechanisms.
    Parameters:
    - total_time: Total simulation time in arbitrary units
    - time_steps_per_unit: Number of steps per unit time
    - All other parameters as defined in the previous simulation functions
    Returns:
    - Arrays of recovered energy, buffer energy levels, feedback effects, and total system energy over time
    """
    total_time_steps = total_time * time_steps_per_unit
    return combined_mechanisms_simulation(
        time_steps=total_time_steps, initial_energy=initial_energy, base_coupling_strength=base_coupling_strength,
        coupling_variation_amplitude=coupling_variation_amplitude, coupling_frequency=coupling_frequency,
        fractal_scaling_factors=fractal_scaling_factors, confinement_lengths=confinement_lengths,
        flux_amplitude=flux_amplitude, flux_frequency=flux_frequency,
        resonance_frequencies=resonance_frequencies, resonance_amplitudes=resonance_amplitudes,
        dissipation_rate=dissipation_rate, recovery_efficiency=recovery_efficiency,
        phase_control_rate=phase_control_rate, disturbance_amplitude=disturbance_amplitude,
        buffer_capacity=buffer_capacity, buffer_discharge_rate=buffer_discharge_rate,
        phase_delay_rate=phase_delay_rate, fast_feedback_rate=fast_feedback_rate, slow_feedback_rate=slow_feedback_rate
    )

# Define long-term simulation parameters
